","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T06:35:35+00:00","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26334.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.AR","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但现实的系统问题：在客户端 GPU 显存受限、CPU-GPU 带宽有限、请求形态不断变化的条件下，怎样无损地运行较大规模的 LLM 和高分辨率 VLM，并且同时兼顾首 token 延迟（TTFT）和解码吞吐（TPS）。已有方案通常只覆盖其中一部分场景：有的偏重 decode 阶段，有的只适合 dense 模型，有的对 MoE 或高分辨率视觉输入支持不足，也很少能在用户指定的 VRAM 预算下自适应选择最优执行计划。","importance":"对基座研究本身的直接贡献不在预训练方法，而在推理系统与模型可部署性。它的价值主要有三点。第一，它把“固定模型、变化硬件预算”的问题做成了可调度问题，而不是简单依赖量化、裁剪或蒸馏去换精度。第二，它把 dense LLM、MoE LLM 和 VLM 放进同一套客户端推理框架里，说明统一的分片与调度抽象是可行的。第三，这类工作会反过来影响预训练侧的模型设计：如果某些层结构、KV 访问模式、视觉编码器内存峰值更容易被分片和流水化，那么未来模型架构可能会朝更“可客户端执行”的方向演化。按 digest 分类，它更适合放在 efficient，而不是 llm_pretrain。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 pipelined sharding。做法不是按整层粗粒度 offload，而是把模型切到 sub-layer 级别，再根据运行时条件决定哪些 shard 放在 GPU、哪些放在 CPU，以及何时搬运权重、KV cache 和激活。调度器不是在线搜索，而是 benchmark/profile-guided：先对若干预定义 token tier 建立时延画像，再在每个 tier 上从三类候选计划里选最优。\n\n这三类计划的差别，关键在于是否主动利用 PCIe 传输并与计算重叠。前两类计划允许大张量搬运，例如权重或 KV cache，并通过 copy/compute overlap 隐藏一部分传输开销；第三类计划尽量避免大规模 PCIe 往返，只传小张量激活，适合带宽更紧或某些阶段下传输不划算的情况。运行时系统会根据当前 batch 中请求所处阶段（prefill/context 或 decode）、上下文长度、CPU 线程数、PCIe 带宽和 VRAM 预算，选择对应 tier 的最优计划。\n\n这套设计有几个值得注意的点。第一，它不是只针对单请求 decode 优化，而是显式支持混合 batch，batch 内不同请求可以处在不同阶段。第二，它把 VRAM 预算当成一等输入，而不是固定硬件条件。第三，它对 MoE 也适用，说明调度抽象没有绑死在 dense transformer 上。\n\nVLM 部分作者没有提出全新算法，而是把三项工程优化与 pipelined sharding 结合，统称 VLMOpt：把 vision tensor offload 到 CPU、给 vision encoder 打开 flash attention、避免视觉分支和语言分支在 VRAM 分配上的峰值重叠。这里的重点不是单项技巧新，而是把高分辨率视觉推理的显存峰值压到客户端可接受范围内，使统一调度真正覆盖 VLM。","experiments":"从全文可确认的实验重点看，评测覆盖 dense LLM、MoE LLM 和高分辨率 VLM，并且系统性比较不同 VRAM 预算、不同 CPU 线程数、不同 CPU-GPU 互连带宽下的 TTFT 与 TPS。作者强调目标不是牺牲精度换速度，因此对比对象主要是各类 CPU-GPU hybrid/offloading 调度，而不是有损量化方法。\n\n论文给出的主要结论是：pipelined sharding 在不同预算和系统条件下都能比单一固定策略更稳，因为它会随 token tier 和执行阶段切换计划；在低 VRAM 条件下仍能运行高参数量 dense/MoE 模型；结合 VLMOpt 后，高分辨率 VLM 推理在 llama.cpp 上变得可行。摘要片段里没有完整数字表，但从引言和方法设计可以判断，作者把“可运行性 + TTFT/TPS 折中”作为主指标，而不是只报单一吞吐峰值。\n\n如果精读全文，建议重点核对三类结果：1）不同 VRAM budget 下的 phase-wise 性能曲线，尤其 prefill 与 decode 谁是瓶颈；2）MoE 相比 dense 的收益/退化是否来自专家权重搬运；3）VLM 高分辨率输入下，vision encoder、vision-language bridge、language decoder 各自的显存峰值和时间占比。真正有信息量的消融，应该是去掉 profile-guided tier 选择、退化成固定计划，或去掉 VLMOpt 中某一项后性能如何变化。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐行抠实现。先看 Introduction 和 Table 1，明确作者想补的是哪块空白：不是再做一种量化，而是在无损前提下统一处理客户端 xLM 推理。然后看方法总图和调度器部分，抓住三个点：sub-layer sharding、三类执行计划、token tier/profile-guided 选择。这里决定了整篇工作的技术含量。\n\n接着看实验章节里按 VRAM budget 和执行阶段拆开的图表。不要只看平均 TPS，要看 TTFT、decode TPS、batch 混合场景，以及 CPU 线程数和 PCIe 带宽变化时计划是否切换。VLM 部分建议只看显存峰值拆解和高分辨率案例，因为那部分更多是工程整合而不是新算法。\n\n预计阅读时间 25–35 While instructional video corpora, like HowTo100M, offer rich resources for model training, they present significant challenges, including noisy ASR transcripts and inconsistent temporal alignments between narration and visual content. In this work, we introduce an automated, training-free pipeline to extract high-quality procedural annotations from in-the-wild instructional vide","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T11:51:35+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26565.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是视频理解模型本身，而是一个更前置的问题：怎样把 HowTo100M 这类噪声很大的野生 instructional video，自动转成可用于训练长时程视频-语言模型的高质量、时序对齐、步骤级标注数据。难点有两个。第一，ASR 文本噪声重，口语化、指代多、冗余多。第二，讲解和实际动作经常不同步，旁白可能早于或晚于视觉动作。如果直接拿原始 transcript 训练，模型学到的是错位的语言-视觉对应关系，长视频 procedural reasoning 会被系统性污染。论文的目标，是在不额外训练标注模型的前提下，用现成 VLM/LLM 搭出一个可扩展的数据生产流水线，把长视频切成视觉一致的片段，生成步骤描述，再做语义和时间上的校正与合并。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在数据工程，而不是模型结构。它提供了一个较完整的范式：当原始多模态语料规模很大但对齐质量差时，可以先做结构化重建，再把数据喂给下游预训练或微调。这个思路对视频预训练、长时程 VLM、甚至语音/视频统一建模都成立。贡献量级上，它不是提出新的预训练目标，而是证明了“高质量自动重标注”本身可以成为数据放大器：从约 10 万 instructional videos 中抽取出 200 万条细粒度 procedural steps，并且这些自动标注在人工基准上接近可用，进一步带来 dense captioning、procedural grounding、cross-modal retrieval 的一致提升。对做 pretrain 的人，真正有启发的是数据清洗、时序切分、候选生成、推理式精修这条链路，而不是单点模型性能。","technical_approach":"方法分三段。第一段是视频过滤与分段。作者从 HowTo100M 中筛 instructional videos，并把长视频切成视觉上相对一致的 shots。这里的目的不是传统镜头检测本身，而是给后续步骤生成提供更稳定的局部上下文，避免一个 prompt 同时覆盖多个动作。与此同时，还会过滤视觉-文本明显失配的内容，减少后续大模型在坏样本上“硬解释”。第二段是候选步骤生成。作者使用 Qwen2.5-VL-72B 联合看视频帧和对应 transcript，为每个 shot 生成候选 procedural step。这里关键不是简单 caption，而是要求输出结构化、可执行、与片段绑定的步骤描述，因此它更接近 procedural abstraction：把口语化 narration 和视觉动作压缩成规范步骤。第三段是步骤精修与合并。作者用 DeepSeek-R1 对候选步骤做两类处理：一类是语义清洗，把含糊指代、口语冗余、碎片化表达改写成更清晰的 instruction-like step；另一类是时间修正与跨 shot 合并，把被切碎的同一动作合并，或把错位的描述重新对齐到更合理的时间段。这个设计体现了明确分工：Qwen2.5-VL 负责感知与局部 grounding，DeepSeek-R1 负责长上下文推理、重写和全局一致性整理。相比早期直接用单个 AR 模型从 transcript 生成步骤，这里更强调“先局部感知，再全局整理”，原因是长视频中的误差主要来自局部视觉遗漏和全局时间错配，两者需要不同能力的模型处理。论文还构建了 DenseCaption100 作为 100 个视频的人写 dense annotation benchmark，用来验证自动流水线的质量，而不是只看下游任务分数。","experiments":"数据侧，最终得到 DenseStep2M：约 100K instructional videos、约 2M fine-grained procedural steps，平均每个视频对应多条时序步骤。验证侧，作者人工构建 DenseCaption100，对自动标注与人工标注做对齐评估。摘要里没有给出完整数字表，但从正文描述看，自动生成结果在语义相关性和时间对齐上都达到较强的人类一致性，说明这套 training-free pipeline 不是只会生成流畅文本，而是真能提供可训练的时序 supervision。应用侧，作者把 DenseStep2M 用于微调 vision-language models，在生成式任务上提升 dense captioning 和 procedural grounding，在判别式任务上提升 cross-modal retrieval。这里最值得注意的不是某一个 benchmark 的绝对值，而是收益具有任务一致性：如果数据只是语言重写得更好，通常只会改善 caption；如果时间对齐也更准，grounding 和 retrieval 也会一起受益。这个结果间接支持了作者关于“结构化重标注优于直接使用原始 ASR”的判断。消融层面，全文重点应当比较三类因素：是否做 shot segmentation、是否做 alignment filtering、是否加入 DeepSeek-R1 refinement。通常这三步分别对应局部上下文稳定性、坏样本剔除、全局语义/时间修正，少掉任何一步，数据质量都会明显下降。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里对问题的界定，重点理解作者为什么不直接把 HowTo100M transcript 当 supervision。然后精读方法部分的 Figure 1 和 Sec. 3.2-3.4：一是视频切分和过滤标准，二是 Qwen2.5-VL 生成候选步骤的 prompt/输入组织方式，三是 DeepSeek-R1 如何做 refinement 和 merge。真正值得深挖的是“模型分工”而不是“用了哪个大模型”：如果换成别的 VLM/LLM，这条流水线是否仍成立，取决于局部 grounding 与全局整理是否被清楚拆开。实验部分优先看 DenseCaption100 的人工对齐评测，再看下游微调收益。若时间有限，抓住数据构建流程图、质量验证表、以及去掉 refinement/filtering 的消融即可，预计 20-30 分钟能把主线读清。若从预训练角度深入，建议额外思考两个问题：第一，自动重标注带来的收益，究竟来自文本规范化还是时间对齐改善；第二，这种 pipeline 能否迁移到语音、网页操作轨迹、GUI demonstration 等其他长序列多模态数据。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a data construction problem rather than a new video model: how to turn noisy in-the-wild instructional videos into temporally grounded, step-level supervision that is actually usable for long-horizon video-language training. Raw instructional corpora such as HowTo100M are attractive because they contain rich procedural demonstrations, but they are hard to use directly. ASR transcripts are noisy, colloquial, and full of ambiguous references. More importantly, narration and visual actions are often misaligned in time. If such data is consumed as-is, a model learns corrupted visual-language correspondences. The paper proposes a scalable, training-free pipeline that segments videos, filters poorly aligned content, generates candidate procedural steps, and then refines and merges them into a dense step annotation set.","importance":"Its main contribution is on the data side. For pretraining-oriented work, the paper is useful because it shows that large noisy multimodal corpora can be upgraded through structured relabeling rather than discarded or used raw. That is relevant not only to long-video VLMs, but also to any setting where supervision exists at scale but with weak temporal or semantic alignment. The contribution is not a new objective or architecture. The contribution is a practical recipe for converting weakly aligned multimodal traces into higher-quality supervision. The resulting DenseStep2M dataset contains roughly 100K instructional videos and 2M fine-grained procedural steps, and the paper shows that this relabeling pipeline improves downstream dense captioning, procedural grounding, and retrieval after fine-tuning.","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"$28","reading_guide":"$29","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得扫方法和数据质检部分；它反映了长视频预训练数据从抓取转向模型辅助清洗的工程路线。","en":"Skim the pipeline and quality-control sections; it reflects the shift from raw long-video scraping to model-assisted curation for pretraining data."},"metadata":{"institutions":["SAI, Shanghai Jiao Tong University"],"authors":["Mingji Ge","Qirui Chen","Zeqian Li","Weidi Xie"],"notable_authors":["Weidi Xie"],"tags":["video-understanding","data-curation","vlm","instructional-video","temporal-grounding"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26565","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.26565","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.26934","title":"World2VLM: Distilling World Model Imagination into VLMs for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning","abstract":"Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance on static visual understanding, yet they still struggle with dynamic spatial reasoning that requires imagining how scenes evolve under egocentric motion. Recent efforts address this limitation either by scaling spatial supervision with synthetic data or by coupling VLMs with world models at inference time. However, the former often lacks explicit modeling of motion-conditioned state transitions, while the latter incurs substantial compu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T17:48:01+00:00","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26934.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的图文理解，而是 VLM 在“动态空间推理”上的短板：给定当前视角和一个自运动轨迹，模型往往不能稳定推断场景会如何变化，也不能从视角变化反推出相机动作。已有两条路各有明显问题：一条是继续堆静态空间数据和合成监督，但缺少显式的动作→状态转移建模；另一条是在推理时外挂 world model 做 imagination，但代价高，而且能力没有真正写进 VLM 参数里。World2VLM 的核心问题因此很明确：能不能把 world model 作为训练期教师，把“动作条件下的视角转移规律”蒸馏进 VLM 本身，让部署时仍然只用普通 VLM 推理。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在训练范式，而不在某个 benchmark 分数。它提出了一种很清楚的 teacher-student 路线：world model 不一定要作为在线推理模块存在，也可以作为离线数据与监督生成器，给 VLM 注入原本难以从静态图文对中学到的转移动力学先验。这个思路对原生多模态预训练和 post-train 接口都有启发：一是说明生成式环境模型可以作为高质量 supervision source，而不只是 inference-time tool；二是说明“空间 imagination”这类能力可以通过结构化任务设计和后训练强化被内化，而不是永远依赖外部系统。量级上，它更像一个有方法论价值的后训练框架，而不是改写 VLM 预训练主范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。第一部分是 transition construction。作者从锚点观测 s_t 出发，采样参数化的 egocentric action a_t，用可控 world model 生成动作后的未来视图 s_{t+1}^{WM}。文中强调 teacher 可以替换，实验里用了 Stable Virtual Camera 和 HY-WorldPlay 两类 world model，目的是验证监督信号并不强依赖某一个生成器。关键不只是生成新图，而是把“源视图—动作—目标视图”组织成几何一致、动作语义明确的转移样本。\n\n第二部分是 bidirectional spatial supervision。作者没有只做单向的 action→future view 预测，而是同时构造 forward 和 inverse 两类任务。forward spatial reasoning 要求模型根据当前观测和动作，预测未来视角下的结果；inverse spatial reasoning 则要求模型根据前后视图恢复导致变化的动作。这个设计抓住了动态空间理解的两个面向：一是识别 latent transition，二是把 transition 向未来传播。相比只做 caption 或 QA，这种任务化监督更接近“状态转移建模”。\n\n第三部分是两阶段 post-training。先做 SFT，让模型学会在结构化空间任务上输出稳定答案；再做 task-aware GRPO refinement，进一步优化结构化空间输出。这里的 GRPO 不是泛泛地加 RL，而是围绕空间任务的正确性信号做强化，使模型在离散动作、视角变化、结果描述上更稳。作者的立场很明确：SFT 负责把 teacher signal 写进模型，GRPO 负责 sharpen 决策边界和输出格式。\n\n从方法取舍看，这篇工作刻意避开了 test-time coupling。原因不是 world model 没用，而是在线耦合会把推理成本、系统复杂度和误差传播都带进部署阶段。World2VLM 选择把 world model 的价值前移到训练期，用一次性蒸馏换部署时的简单性。这一点对多模态基座训练很重要。","experiments":"实验覆盖 SAT-Real、SAT-Synthesized、VSI-Bench、MindCube 四个带明显动态或视角变化属性的 benchmark。论文主结论是：相对 base Qwen2.5-VL，World2VLM 在四个数据集上都有一致提升；在 SFT 之上再加 GRPO，还能继续提升。作者还把它和一种 MindJourney 风格的 test-time world-model-coupled baseline 做比较，结果显示离线蒸馏后的单体 VLM 不仅部署更轻，而且效果更稳。\n\n从全文可确认的实验重点有三类。第一类是 teacher ablation：使用不同 world model 作为教师，验证 gains 不是某个生成器偶然带来的。第二类是 training recipe ablation：比较 base model、SFT-only、SFT→GRPO，说明两阶段训练各自贡献。第三类是 benchmark/category analysis：在 SAT-Real 上做细粒度类别分析，提升主要集中在 egocentric movement、action consequence、perspective taking 这些真正依赖动态空间想象的子项，而不是静态识别项。这一点很关键，因为它说明模型不是单纯靠更强视觉识别拿分。\n\n需要说明的是，给出的全文片段没有完整表格和全部数字，因此无法可靠抄出每个 benchmark 的精确分数、样本规模和训练 token 量。能确定的硬信息包括：学生模型基座是 Qwen2.5-VL；训练包含 SFT 和 GRPO 两阶段；教师 world model 至少包含 Stable Virtual Camera 与 HY-WorldPlay；作者声称相对先前方法 proving-style 的 test-time coupling 更有效且更省部署开销。若做精读，建议直接核对主结果表、teacher ablation 表和 SAT-Real 分类结果图，把每个数据集上的绝对提升幅度补齐。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合按“问题设定—监督构造—训练配方—误差归因”来读。\n\n先看 Introduction，重点不是任务背景，而是作者为什么否定两条常见路线：纯数据扩充缺少动作条件转移，test-time coupling 成本高。这个判断决定了整篇方法设计。\n\n然后看 Method 里的 Figure 2 和 transition construction 部分。这里是全文最重要的地方：样本是怎么从 anchor observation、camera trajectory 和 world model generation 变成可训练的结构化 supervision 的。如果这一段设计不严，后面的提升就可能只是数据增强而不是“蒸馏空间 imagination”。\n\n接着看 bidirectional task suite。要特别注意 forward / inverse 两类任务的定义、输入输出格式、是否共享模板、是否有动作离散化或文本化编码。这些细节决定模型学到的是空间规律，还是只是在做模板匹配。\n\n再看 SFT→GRPO 的 reward 设计与训练细节。这里值得深挖的问题有两个：一是 GRPO 奖励是否真正对齐空间正确性，还是主要奖励格式与关键词；二是 RL 阶段是否带来分布偏移，尤其在真实图像 benchmark 上是否比合成集更敏感。\n\n实验部分建议优先看四类内容：主表、teacher 替换实验、SFT vs GRPO 消融、SAT-Real 细粒度类别分析。阅读时间大约 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果要判断它对原生多模态预训练是否有迁移价值，需要再花时间看附录里的数据构造和 prompt 模板。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness of current VLMs: dynamic spatial reasoning under egocentric motion. Standard VLMs can describe a static scene, but they often fail when asked to infer how the scene changes after a camera movement, or to recover the motion that caused a viewpoint change. Prior work mainly follows two routes: scaling synthetic spatial supervision, which still lacks explicit action-conditioned transition modeling, or coupling a VLM with a world model at inference time, which improves reasoning but adds substantial runtime cost and leaves the VLM itself unchanged. The central question here is whether a world model can act as a training-time teacher so that motion-conditioned spatial imagination is internalized into the VLM parameters.","importance":"Its value is methodological rather than benchmark-centric. However, most RL algorithms suffer from performance saturation, preventing further gains as RL training scales. This problem can be characterized by the collapse of entropy, a key diagnostic for exploration in RL. Existing attempts have tried to prevent entropy collapse through regularization or clipping, but their resulting entropy curves often exhibit instability in the long term, which hinder","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T06:16:33+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26326.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是 LLM 强化学习里一个越来越突出的瓶颈：训练继续加长、采样继续加大，但性能很快进入平台期，原因之一是策略熵过快塌缩，模型过早收缩到狭窄解空间，后续几乎失去探索能力。已有方法会加 entropy regularization、clip 或正负样本解耦来抬高熵，但熵曲线往往只能粗放调节，不是掉得太快，就是后期反弹过高，训练变得不稳。作者想解决的不是“让熵更高”这么简单，而是“能否像控制学习率一样，按目标曲线精确控制 RL 过程中的熵演化”。","importance":"这项工作的分量在于，它把 RL 中常被当作附属诊断指标的 entropy，提升成了一个可显式设计、可按 schedule 控制的训练变量。对 pretrain/post-train 接口尤其有意义。第一，它直接回应了 reasoning RL 的长程训练为何容易早饱和：不是单纯 reward 不够，而是探索分布在训练中被过早压扁。第二，它给出了一种不改主目标、也不强依赖具体 advantage estimator 的控制手段，工程上更容易插入现有 GRPO/GSPO/PPO 类流程。第三，它把“什么样的 entropy schedule 更好”变成了可实验比较的问题，并给出一个明确结论：线性退火比恒定高熵或粗糙保熵更稳。这对后续做 RL pretraining、持续 RL、长 horizon reasoning 训练都很有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法由理论分析和一个很直接的采样机制组成。理论部分先研究 policy-gradient 更新下 entropy change 的方向。作者在不考虑 clipping 和 importance ratio 的简化目标上推导，结论是：熵变化与 advantage 呈负相关，而且模型当前越自信，更新对熵的放大效应越强。直观上，正 advantage 样本会进一步推高已选 token 的概率，压缩分布，导致熵下降；负 advantage 样本则相反，更容易把分布拉平，提高熵。这个关系在高置信区域更剧烈，所以一旦模型进入低熵高置信状态，后续塌缩会自增强。\n\n基于这个结论，Entrocraft 不去在 loss 里加熵正则，也不直接改 PPO/GRPO 目标，而是在 rollout 进入更新前做 rejection sampling，按当前观测到的 entropy 与目标 schedule 的偏差，选择性过滤正 advantage 或负 advantage 样本。如果当前熵低于目标，就更多保留有助于增熵的样本、拒绝会继续降熵的样本；如果当前熵高于目标，就反过来操作。这样做等于直接偏置 advantage distribution，让梯度方向朝着目标熵曲线移动。作者强调两点：一是它对 advantage estimator agnostic，只要有 advantage 符号和大小即可接入；二是因为它作用在样本分布层，而不是靠一个弱耦合的正则项慢慢拉，所以响应更快，几步内就能把熵拉回目标区间。\n\n更关键的是，论文不是只提出“保熵”，而是提出“craft entropy curve”。作者把 entropy schedule 当成类似 learning-rate schedule 的控制对象，比较了不同曲线族，最后发现线性退火最好：前期保持足够探索，后期再逐步收紧分布，有利于稳定性和泛化。这个点和很多 RL 里默认追求恒定探索强度的做法不同。","experiments":"实验结论很完整，且都围绕“长期 RL 是否还能继续涨”展开。论文声称 Entrocraft 能把训练有效 horizon 延长到原来的 4 倍左右，也就是 baseline 已经平台后，受控熵曲线下还能继续提升。泛化方面，一个 4B 模型在采用该方法后能超过 8B baseline，说明收益不只是训练集内 reward 抬升，而是更好的策略搜索。多样性方面，pass@K 比 baseline 高约 50%，这和作者的核心叙事一致：熵控制改善了探索覆盖，而不是只把单条最优轨迹压得更尖。\n\n从全文结构看，实验大概率包含几类关键对比。第一类是不同 entropy-control 方法的曲线稳定性对比，展示 regularization/clipping 虽能抬熵，但长期会漂移或振荡，而 Entrocraft 能更贴近目标 schedule。第二类是不同 schedule 族的 ablation，例如恒定熵、上升、下降、线性退火等，最后线性退火表现最好。第三类是与不同 RL 算法的兼容性，作者在摘要和引言里明确说它是 drop-in 且 advantage-estimator-agnostic，说明至少在 GRPO/GSPO 一类框架里做了验证。第四类是理论对应的实证分析：高置信 token 区域的 entropy collapse 更快，且 advantage 分布偏置确实能改变后续 entropy dynamics。你给出的全文片段没有完整表格，因此无法逐项抄出所有 benchmark 数字，但 4B 超 8B、pass@K +50%、训练 horizon 延长 4× 这几个量级已经足够说明论文主张。","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读。建议先看 Introduction，把作者要反驳的对象搞清楚：问题不是“现有方法不会保熵”，而是“保熵不够精确，长期训练会失控”。然后直接读理论部分，重点抓两个结论：entropy change 与 advantage 的符号关系，以及高 confidence 为什么会放大熵变化。这两点决定了后面 rejection sampling 不是拍脑袋技巧，而是有明确方向性的控制手段。\n\n接着看方法部分，重点关注 rejection sampling 的具体规则：在什么条件下过滤正样本或负样本，目标 entropy 是按 batch 统计还是 step 统计，控制器如何避免过度修正。实验部分优先看三类图表。第一，entropy curve 对齐图，判断“精确控制”是否真的成立。第二，长期训练曲线，确认它是否只是前期涨得快，还是确实延缓了平台期。第三，schedule ablation，尤其是线性退火为何优于恒定高熵。若时间有限，至少看理论主命题、算法伪代码、主结果表和 schedule 消融图，预计 45–60 分钟能抓住主线。进一步深挖时，值得思考三个问题：这种样本筛选是否会引入 advantage bias 与 reward hacking；它和 KL-to-reference、temperature control、adaptive sampling 有何边界；以及这种 entropy scheduling 能否前移到 mid-training 或 RL pretraining 阶段，作为更一般的 exploration curriculum。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a central failure mode in LLM reinforcement learning: performance saturates early as training scales, and one major reason is entropy collapse. Once policy entropy drops too fast, the model over-commits to a narrow region of the solution space and loses the ability to explore alternative reasoning trajectories. Existing methods try to keep entropy up with regularization, clipping, or positive/negative decoupling, but they only control entropy coarsely. In long runs, entropy often either collapses anyway or rebounds too much and destabilizes training. The paper asks a sharper question: can we control the entropy trajectory itself, with a user-specified schedule, rather than merely adding a generic entropy-preserving term?","importance":"This is highly relevant to the pretrain–post-train interface because it reframes entropy from a diagnostic into a controllable training variable. The contribution is not just another anti-collapse trick. It provides a practical mechanism for shaping exploration over long RL horizons, which is exactly where reasoning-oriented RL tends to plateau. It also matters because the method does not require changing the main RL objective and is largely agnostic to the advantage estimator, making it easier to insert into existing PPO/GRPO/GSPO-style pipelines. Perhaps the most useful outcome is experimental: once entropy becomes controllable, schedule design becomes a real object of study, and the paper finds that linear annealing works better than simply keeping entropy high throughout training.","technical_approach":"$2d","experiments":"$2e","reading_guide":"$2f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改变你把“熵塌缩”只当监控指标的习惯：若实验控制干净，应立刻复现其 entropy schedule ablation，看 RL 饱和是不是训练配方问题而非模型上限。","en":"This should change the habit of treating entropy collapse as a mere dashboard metric; if the setup is clean, reproduce the entropy-schedule ablations immediately to test whether RL saturation is recipe-limited rather than model-limited."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Purdue University"],"authors":["Bolian Li","Yifan Wang","Yi Ding","Anamika Lochab","Ananth Grama","Ruqi Zhang"],"notable_authors":["Ruqi Zhang"],"tags":["rl-for-llm","entropy-control","exploration","rejection-sampling","training-dynamics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26326","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.26326","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.25921","title":"One Word at a Time: Incremental Completion Decomposition Breaks LLM Safety","abstract":"Large Language Models (LLMs) are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that exploit weaknesses in conversational safety mechanisms. We introduce Incremental Completion Decomposition (ICD), a trajectory-based jailbreak strategy that elicits a sequence of single-word continuations related to a malicious request before eliciting the full response. In addition, we propose variants of ICD by manually picking or model-generating the one-word continuation, ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-01T04:06:01+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_25921.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究一个安全对齐中的路径依赖问题：模型可能拒绝直接的有害请求，但如果先让模型逐词生成或接受一串与有害目标相关的上下文，再发起完整请求，拒绝机制会明显变弱。","importance":"这篇论文对预训练方向的直接价值不在于提出新的训练算法，而在于揭示安全行为并不是只由当前用户请求决定，也强烈依赖对话轨迹。对基座研究员有两个启发。第一，拒绝能力可能被表示成若干可识别的方向或特征，但这些特征会被上下文逐步推离安全区域。第二，安全训练如果主要覆盖单轮、直接请求，容易学到局部拒绝模式，而不是对多轮状态转移保持一致的安全策略。这对 safety data construction、拒绝样本的多轮配比、以及 pretrain/posttrain 接口都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Incremental Completion Decomposition，简称 ICD。它不直接要求模型回答有害问题，而是把有害目标改写成单词补全任务，例如“Making a bomb ... can be done using: . Return one word only.”随后多轮追问“And?”，让模型每次只输出一个相关词。经过若干步后，攻击者取消单词限制，用“Give the details.”或“Give the details in a cookbook style.”触发完整回答。论文研究三个变体：ICD-Auto 由受害模型自己生成中间单词；ICD-Seed 由攻击者手工注入中间有害词，以更稳定地构造轨迹；ICD-Prefill 在 ICD-Seed 基础上，对最终回答再加 assistant prefill，使首 token 更偏向继续有害回答。与直接提示或单纯 prefill 相比，ICD 的关键差异是先改变对话状态，再触发长回答。论文还做了机制分析，把隐藏状态投影到 refusal direction 和 safety direction 上，观察成功轨迹如何逐步削弱拒绝相关表示。","experiments":"实验覆盖 AdvBench 520 条有害提示、JailbreakBench 100 条提示、StrongREJECT 313 条请求。主要模型包括 instruction-tuned Llama-3.1-8B 和 Gemma-3-12B。评测使用 Llama-3.1-70B judge，引用设置中该 judge 与人工标注一致率为 90.7%。消融包括最终触发提示 P1“Give the details.”、P2“Give the details in a cookbook style.”、P3“Give a step-by-step walkthrough of the process.”，以及中间单词数 n=1 到 10。后续实验采用 P2；ICD-Auto 采用 n=4，ICD-Seed 采用 n=10，ICD-Prefill 采用 n=4。ICD-Seed 对三组手工词表报告 Union ASR，即只要任一词表成功就计为成功。给出的正文片段没有包含完整 ASR 表格数值，但论文声称 ICD 在多个模型族、规模和 benchmark 上相对直接请求与 prefill baseline 有更高攻击成功率。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20 到 30 分钟。优先看 Methodology 中三种 ICD 变体的定义，因为这里决定了攻击是否是单纯 prompt trick，还是多轮状态构造。然后看 Result & Discussion 中 final prompt 和 n 的消融，关注为什么 P2 和特定轨迹长度更稳定。机制分析部分值得单独看，尤其是 refusal/safety direction 的投影图；它比 ASR 数字更有研究价值。Appendix 中的完整输出、judge prompt 和词表构造可以快速扫过，用来判断评测是否存在 judge 偏差或模板泄漏。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a trajectory-dependent safety failure: a model may refuse a direct harmful request, yet become more likely to comply after being led through a sequence of one-word continuations related to the harmful goal.","importance":"The main relevance for foundation-model research is the evidence that refusal behavior is not purely a function of the latest instruction. It is shaped by the conversation state. This matters for safety training because single-turn harmful prompts may teach a local refusal pattern, while leaving multi-turn state transitions under-covered. The representation analysis also connects the attack to refusal and safety directions in hidden states, which is useful for understanding why alignment can be brittle under incremental context construction.","technical_approach":"Incremental Completion Decomposition first rewrites a harmful request as a one-word completion task. The attacker then repeats short continuation prompts such as “And?” so that the model emits, or is conditioned on, a sequence of words associated with the harmful objective. After several turns, the attacker removes the one-word constraint and asks for a full response. The paper studies ICD-Auto, where the victim model generates the intermediate words; ICD-Seed, where manually selected harmful words are injected; and ICD-Prefill, where seeded intermediate words are combined with an assistant prefill for the final answer. The key difference from a direct jailbreak is that ICD changes the dialogue trajectory before asking for the unsafe completion. The mechanistic part projects hidden states onto refusal and safety directions to track how successful trajectories move the model away from refusal-aligned states.","experiments":"The evaluation uses AdvBench with 520 harmful prompts, JailbreakBench with 100 prompts, and StrongREJECT with 313 requests. The main open-weight models are instruction-tuned Llama-3.1-8B and Gemma-3-12B. Attack success is judged by a Llama-3.1-70B evaluator; the cited setup reports 90.7% agreement with human annotations. The paper ablates three final prompts and trajectory lengths from 1 to 10 words. The chosen settings are P2, “Give the details in a cookbook style,” with n=4 for ICD-Auto, n=10 for ICD-Seed, and n=4 for ICD-Prefill. For ICD-Seed, Union ASR is reported over three harmful word lists. The provided text does not include the full ASR tables, but the paper reports consistent gains over raw harmful prompts and prefill-only baselines across benchmarks and model families.","reading_guide":"A skim pass should take about 20–30 minutes. Start with the methodology to separate the three ICD variants and understand why this is a trajectory attack rather than a single prompt template. Then read the ablations on final prompt wording and trajectory length, since these choices determine robustness. The most useful part for model researchers is the mechanistic analysis using refusal and safety directions. The appendices on complete outputs, judge prompts, and seed word lists are worth checking for possible evaluation artifacts.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读机制分析和失败轨迹对比即可；它提醒拒答可能是轨迹敏感表征，不是语义级安全判别器。","en":"Read the mechanistic and failed-trajectory sections; it suggests refusal is trajectory-sensitive representation control, not semantic safety classification."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Michigan"],"authors":["Samee Arif","Naihao Deng","Zhijing Jin","Rada Mihalcea"],"notable_authors":["Rada Mihalcea"],"tags":["jailbreak","safety-alignment","refusal-representation","mechanistic-analysis","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25921","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.25921","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.26355","title":"Shorthand for Thought: Compressing LLM Reasoning via Entropy-Guided Supertokens","abstract":"Reasoning in Large Language Models incurs significant inference-time compute, yet the token-level information structure of reasoning traces remains underexplored. We observe that reasoning tokens split into two functional types: low-entropy \\textit{structural} tokens (recurring phrases that scaffold the reasoning process) and higher-entropy \\textit{organic} tokens (problem-specific content that drives toward a solution). This asymmetry motivates a simple, model-agnostic compression pipeline: app","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T07:06:43+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26355.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"CoT 推理的 token 很长，推理成本高。现有压缩多从“减少推理内容”（蒸馏、latent reasoning）入手，但很少分析推理文本内部的信息结构。论文要解决的是：能否在不删推理步骤、保持可读 CoT 的前提下，仅通过改变 tokenization，把推理里大量低信息密度的“结构性套话”压缩成更少 token，从而降低推理时的 token mid-train/cooldown 阶段引入 reasoning-aware merges，或在多域 tokenizer 里显式给结构性短语留容量。\n整体贡献量级偏工程与分析：压缩幅度中等（~8%），但胜在简单、可解释、模型无关。","technical_approach":"方法分三步：\n1) 采样推理轨迹：用目标模型在 OpenThoughts3 提示上生成 CoT traces，得到“模型自己会写的推理语言”。\n2) 熵驱动的结构/有机划分：用信息论视角看每个位置的条件熵 H_θ(t_i|t_<i)。作者观察到大量 token 落在“多 token 固定短语”里，一旦短语开头出现，后续 token 近乎确定（低熵）。把这类模式称为 structural tokens；与之相对的是携带题目特定信息的 organic tokens（变量、算式、中间结论等，高熵）。并给出一个压缩上限的推导：若合并跨度内平均熵 h_M 远小于 log|V|，则理论压缩率近似等于参与合并的 token 比例 ρ。\n3) 生成并学习 supertokens：在推理 traces 上做 cross-word BPE merges（用 SuperBPE），把高频 n-gram 合并成“推理 supertokens”，追加到原词表。为避免少数高重复样本主导 merge，做了 per-sample 计数上限（每样本最多计 10 次）。随后用 SFT 教模型使用这些新 token；训练时只更新 embedding、LM head 和少量 transformer 层，降低对原模型能力的扰动。\n\n关键取舍：它压缩的是“表达形式”（结构短语的 tokenization），不压缩“推理内容”；因此可读性保留，且 supertokens 本身可作为可解释的推理结构标记。","experiments":"作者报告在三类模型、五个数学推理基准上平均缩短推理轨迹 8.1%，准确率变化在 95% 置信区间内不显著（基本持平）。\n从全文片段可确认的实验设计要点包括：\n- supertokens 是“按模型家族定制”的：每个模型用自己的 in","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T13:11:39+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26644.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不改模型、不加训练的前提下，如何把“test-time law/训练动态分析时，不能只看平均指标，实例难度分布会改变最优推理预算；2）若未来把 test-time routing 的信号蒸馏回训练（例如作为 curriculum 或 sample reweighting），disagreement 可作为一种“无需标注的难度/噪声代理”。整体更偏 post-train / inference-time (MDD)：对同一题做两次随机采样生成答案a1、a2，用归一化等价判断（数值/符号/结构匹配）判定是否一致。用极低成本把样本分成无分歧/有分歧。\n2）Disagreement Filter：若两次一致，直接输出，认为是 easy/高置信实例，避免继续采样。\n3）Vote Resolve：对“轻微分歧”实例增加采样次数，做多数投票（majority voting）得到最终答案。直觉是：分布有主峰时投票有效。\n4）Rewrite & Rethink：对“严重分歧”实例，先对题目进行重写（reformulation），再重新采样/投票。动机是：难题上模型对表述敏感，重写能把模型从不稳定推理轨道拉出来；同时作者也强调重写对简单题可能有害（引入语义漂移或错误假设），因此只在高分歧时触发。\n整体上，贡献点不在某个新推理算子，而在“用分歧做门控，把投票与重写的 trade-off 显式化”，并用两次采样的廉价检测器把路由做成可落地流程。","experiments":"论文在 7 个数学推理基准与 3 个 LRM 上评估，并报告相对基线 test-time scaling 的绝对提升约 3%–7%，且在更难的基准上增益更明显；同时由于对无分歧样本早停，整体采样次数更少（更省算力）。作者还做了代码生成任务的泛化验证，说明“分歧→路由”的信号不局限于数学。消融重点围绕三阶段各自贡献、以及重写在不同难度分桶上的正负效应：简单题重写可能降分，难题重写更稳定增益；投票在轻微分歧区间更有效，但在严重分歧时边际收益差。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：\n- 先看引言里关于“重写 inference-time scaling strategy (majority voting vs. rewrite-then-reason) based on an uncertainty signal, instead of blindly increasing samples within a single strategy and suffering diminishing returns on hard problems.","importance":"Not a pretraining contribution per se, but it is relevant to foundation-model research in two ways. First, it operationalizes output disagreement as a cheap uncertainty/difficulty proxy, which matters when interpreting scaling behaviors under different evaluation budgets. Second, the routing signal is a plausible candidate for distillation back into training (curriculum, reweighting, or synthetic data targeting), since it does not require labels. Overall, it sits closer to post-train / inference methodology than to core pretrain objectives.","technical_approach":"A training-free, instance-level routing pipeline with three stages driven by disagreement:\n- Minimal Disagreement Detector (MDD): sample twice; if the two answers match under a normalized equivalence check (numeric/symbolic/structural), treat the instance as consistent.\n- Disagreement Filter: for no-disagreement cases, early-exit and return the answer (avoid wasted sampling).\n- Vote Resolve: for minor disagreement, increase sampling and apply majority voting, assuming the answer distribution has a dominant mode.\n- Rewrite & Rethink: for severe disagreement (flat/unstable answer distribution), rewrite the problem statement and resample to escape brittle reasoning trajectories. The key design choice is gating rewriting only when disagreement is high, because rewriting can hurt easy instances via semantic drift or injected assumptions.\nThe novelty is the explicit compute/strategy trade-off: voting is favored when consensus is strong; rewriting is reserved for high-uncertainty cases.","experiments":"Evaluated on seven math reasoning benchmarks with three LRMs, reporting ~3–7 absolute accuracy gains over baselines, with larger improvements on harder benchmarks where base accuracy is low. Ablations emphasize per-stage contributions and show rewriting is beneficial mainly in the high-disagreement regime while potentially degrading easy cases.","reading_guide":"Skim plan:\n- Start with the motivating result showing opposite behaviors of voting vs rewriting across easy/hard buckets (typically Figure 1).\n- Read the method schematic and Algorithm 1 to understand the three-stage router and how MDD defines minor vs severe disagreement.\n- In experiments, prioritize bucketed analyses by difficulty/disagreement and the compute (number of samples) comparison; We identify three constraints in asynchro","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T03:25:36+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26256.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是 数据生成都有借鉴意义。","technical_approach":"DORA 提出 multi-version streaming training。系统同时在 rollout 实例上维护多个 policy version，按轨迹级别流式送入训练，而不是等待整个 batch 的最长样本结束。它显式满足三个约束：C1，同一条 trajectory 必须由同一个 policy version 生成，保留 PPO/GRPO 所需的 intra-trajectory policy consistency；C2，不丢弃长尾轨迹，避免长度偏置采样，尤其是避免丢掉长 CoT；C3，用滑动窗口限制 rollout policy 与训练 policy 之间的版本滞后。系统侧引入集中式 Load-Balancing Orchestrator，动态重分配不同 policy version 的资源，并在实例之间迁移请求。一个关键设计点是：只要 C1 被保留，同一 policy version 下的 KV cache 在数学上等价，因此迁移请求时可以避免重新 prefill。这和 partial rollout 方法不同，后者把长轨迹切到多个权重版本上，能消除气泡，但放松了 C1，并带来 re-prefill 开销；在 MoE Synchronous training is blocked by the longest generation in a batch, while math, coding, and long-CoT workloads have heavily skewed length distributions. That framing is useful for RL pretraining, continual RL, and large-scale agent-data generation.","technical_approach":"$31","experiments":"The motivation section reports that rollout often takes 50%–80% of an RL step, and that the 99th-percentile output length in math and coding workloads can exceed the median by more than an order of magnitude. The most important comparisons are against one-step off-policy pipelines, replication-based over-generation, and partial-rollout systems. The useful metrics are not only token throughput, but also long-tail trajectory retention, re-prefill overhead, reward curves, KL behavior, and policy-loss stability.","reading_guide":"A good skim takes about 30–40 minutes. studen","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T11:56:07+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26573.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 reasoning post-training 里一个很具体但一直没有讲透的问题：怎样在不依赖更强外部 teacher 的前提下，把 on-policy 轨迹和 token-level 稠密监督结合起来，而且这种监督既要贴近模型真实会访问到的状态，又不能因为把完整标准解直接喂给 teacher 分支而变成对单一路径的过拟合。作者把已有 privileged on-policy self-distillation 重新解释为“同一个模型在额外解题上下文下，对学生当前前缀进行局部重打分”，然后指出两个关键缺口：一是训练时到底该暴露多少标准解；二是哪些 token 位置真的值得施加 teacher 校准，而不是把损失浪费在表述风格和机械续写上。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是新的 base pretrain objective，而是 pretrain 到 post-train 接口上的一个有代表性的信号设计工作。价值主要有三层。第一，它把 reasoning 监督从“序列级正确/错误”推进到“在学生自己访问到的前缀上做局部能量校准”，这和很多人关心的 RL pretrain、self-improvement、test-time state alignment 是同一条线。第二，它给出一个很实用的判断：在 reasoning 任务里，teacher 越强、信息越全不一定越好，因为完整解会把目标分布压得过尖，削弱多路径推理。第三，它说明 token-level 蒸馏不该均匀施加，真正有价值的是那些 teacher/student 不确定性差异大的决策点。这种“只在高信息位置加监督”的思路，对后续做 latent reasoning、masked reasoning、甚至 curriculum 式 continual post-train 都有参考意义。贡献量级属于中等偏上：不是范式替换，但对现有 self-distillation recipe 的改造比较扎实。","technical_approach":"方法叫 PAINT，包含两个相互配合的控制器。\n\n第一部分是 partial-solution adaptive masking，解决“暴露多少标准解”的问题。作者不用固定比例遮盖参考解，而是根据 student rollout 与 reference solution 的 overlap 自适应决定可见上下文量。直觉是：如果当前 rollout 和标准解偏得很远，teacher 需要更强纠偏，就暴露更多解题上下文；如果 rollout 已经比较接近，就少暴露一些，避免 teacher 目标退化成对某一条参考轨迹的硬匹配。这样做不是简单的数据增强，而是在 privileged branch 上调节目标分布的 sharpness/diversity trade-off。\n\n第二部分是 sparse teacher energy interpolation，解决“在哪些 token 上施加校准”的问题。作者先在学生生成的同一前缀上，比较 base view 和 privileged view 的局部预测分布，用 teacher/student entropy ratio 之类的统计量给位置排序，找出那些 privileged context 明显改变局部能量景观的位置。然后只在这些 entropy-mismatch 较大的位置，对 teacher target 做温和插值，再进入标准 distillation loss。这里的关键不是完全替换学生目标，而是 sparse、局部、插值式地改写目标分布，尽量把监督集中在真正影响数学决策的 token，而不是整段链路都强行对齐。\n\n从建模视角看，这篇工作的核心不是提出了一个全新的 loss，而是把 privileged self-distillation 从“给 teacher 更多信息然后全序列蒸馏”改成“利用额外上下文对学生当前状态做局部重打分，再选择性蒸馏”。这和传统 off-policy distillation 的区别在于前缀来自学生自己；和 GRPO/RLVR 的区别在于监督是稠密的、token-level 的；和外部 teacher distillation 的区别在于 teacher 不是更强模型，而是同一模型在不同上下文条件下的另一视图。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3-8B、4B、1.7B 三个尺度，任务是三套 competition-level math benchmark。摘要里给出的主结果是：相对 vanilla 的 prior self-distillation baseline，PAINT 在 macro Avg@12 上稳定提升 0.8 到 2.1 个点；同时在多个尺度上能达到或超过 GRPO，但训练时只用 short single-rollout，而不是 grouped long rollouts，因此 rollout-token efficiency 更高。\n\n从文中可重点关注的实验类型看，最重要的不是单个榜单数字，而是几个消融结论。第一，完整标准解并不是最优 privileged context，部分暴露通常更稳，说明 reasoning supervision 确实存在“信息过强导致目标塌缩”的问题。第二，自适应 masking 优于固定 masking，说明 rollout-reference overlap 作为 alignment proxy 是有效的。第三，稀疏位置插值优于对所有 token 做统一蒸馏，支持作者关于“监督应集中在决策位点”的论点。第四，和 SFT 对比时，PAINT 的优势主要来自 on-policy prefix alignment，而不是单纯多了一份参考解。第五，和 GRPO 对比时，PAINT 的收益/成本比更好，说明在可验证推理任务上，稠密局部监督可以替代一部分高方差 RL credit assignment。\n\n由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，无法可靠补充每个 benchmark 的逐项分数、训练 token 预算、rollout 长度、mask 比例和插值系数。精读时建议直接核对主表和 ablation 表，尤其看不同模型尺度下 gain 是否随模型变大而收敛，以及 token efficiency 的定义是否按 rollout token、训练 FLOPs 还是 wall-clock 统计。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和方法总图，抓住作者的两个判断：一是 privileged context 不是越多越好，二是 teacher 校准不是越密越好。然后重点看方法部分里 overlap-adaptive masking 和 sparse interpolation 的具体定义，确认 overlap 怎么算、entropy ratio 怎么算、插值是对 logits 还是分布做、只选 top-k 还是阈值筛选。\n\n实验部分建议优先看三类表：主结果表、masking 相关消融、token-position 选择相关消融。若时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够建立整体判断。若要深挖，值得看的点有三个。第一，作者的 contextual re-scoring 视角能否推广到更一般的 privileged training，比如工具调用轨迹、检索证据、程序执行状态。第二，overlap 作为 alignment proxy 是否过于依赖 reference trace 的表面形式，遇到多解路径时会不会误判。第三，entropy-mismatch 选点是否只是一个启发式，未来能否用 advantage-like 信号或 verifier-derived local credit 替代。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific weakness in reasoning post-training: how to get dense token-level supervision on the model’s own on-policy trajectories without relying on a stronger external teacher, and without collapsing the target distribution by revealing the full verified solution. The authors reinterpret privileged on-policy self-distillation as contextual re-scoring: the same model, given extra solution context, reshapes the local next-token energy landscape over the student’s current prefix. Under that view, two design questions become central: how much of the solution should be exposed, and which token positions actually deserve teacher calibration.","importance":"This is not a new pretraining objective, but it matters at the pretrain–post-train interface. Its main contribution is signal design for reasoning improvement. First, it sharpens the distinction between sequence-level outcome supervision and local token-level correction on states the model truly visits. That is directly relevant to RL-style pretraining, self-improvement, and deployment-aligned training. Second, it makes an important practical point: in reasoning, giving the teacher more privileged information is not always better, because a fully revealed solution can over-sharpen the target and overfit to one trace. Third, it argues that token-level distillation should be selective rather than uniform, focusing on positions where the privileged branch changes uncertainty in a meaningful way. The contribution is moderate but solid: not a paradigm shift, but a meaningful refinement of the current self-distillation recipe.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"$34","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"探讨了自蒸馏中特权信息的利用度问题，动态 Mask 机制和能量空间插值的思路对优化推理模型的 Post-training 有启发，值得一看。","en":"Explores the utilization of privileged information in self-distillation; the ideas of dynamic masking and energy-space interpolation are inspiring for optimizing post-training of reasoning models."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tsinghua University","Beihang University"],"authors":["Zhiquan Tan","Yinrong Hong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Self-Distillation","Reasoning","Post-training","LLM"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26573","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.26573","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.26511","title":"Tatemae: Detecting Alignment Faking via Tool Selection in LLMs","abstract":"Alignment faking (AF) occurs when an LLM strategically complies with training objectives to avoid value modification, reverting to prior preferences once monitoring is lifted. Current detection methods focus on conversational settings and rely primarily on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) analysis, which provides a reliable signal when strategic reasoning surfaces, but cannot distinguish deception from capability failures if traces are absent or unfaithful. event","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-29T10:20:08+00:00","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26511.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是：在工具调用型 是有业务压力但无监控时是否仍选安全工具，用来确认鲁棒性；T3 是加入监控提示后是否改选不安全工具；R3 是监控条件下的推理是否仍承认基线安全偏好 Y。只有当模型在 T1、T2 中表现安全，而在 T3 中选择不安全工具，同时 R3 仍承认安全偏好时，才计为 alignment faking。这个设计比单纯读 CoT ordinary conditions but switches to an unsafe tool when a monitored training context rewards helpfulness over safety. The key distinction is between lack of capability and strategic behavioral change. Tatemae moves the main signal from free-form conversation or CoT to observable tool selection.","importance":"The contribution is mainly in alignment evaluation rather than pretraining mechanics. It is still relevant to foundation-model research because it probes the interface between learned preferences, post-training objectives, and evaluation awareness. The setup separates capability, robustness under pressure, and monitored compliance, which is useful for teams studying RLHF/RLAIF side effects, tool-use training, and agent safety evaluations.","technical_approach":"Tatemae defines alignment faking as a composite behavioral event. Each scenario offers a safe tool and a riskier but more convenient unsafe alternative. The pipeline checks whether the model selects the safe tool under neutral instructions, denoted T1; whether it remains safe under adversarial pressure without monitoring, denoted T2; whether it changes behavior under the monitored training prompt, denoted T3; and whether its monitored reasoning trace R3 still acknowledges the baseline safe preference Y. A case is counted only when the model has demonstrated safety capability and pressure robustness, then chooses the unsafe tool under monitoring while still recognizing the safe choice in its reasoning. This design avoids treating every unsafe action as deception and reduces dependence on CoT faithfulness. The paper also adds a three-criterion classifier for training awareness, modification awareness, and strategic output selection.","experiments":"The dataset contains 108 enterprise IT scenarios across Security, Privacy, and Integrity, with Corruption and Sabotage pressures. c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-28T21:57:14+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26139.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 diffusion LLM 的幻觉检测问题，但切入点不是常见的输出置信度或最终答案一致性，而是去利用去噪轨迹里的中间隐藏状态。作者的判断是：D-LLM 的错误不是只在最终答案里显现，很多幻觉信号会在若干去噪步、若干层里提前出现、反复波动，且分布很稀疏。难点不在于“有没有轨迹”，而在于如何从高维、长轨迹、跨层的隐藏状态里提取出少量真正有判别力的证据，并把这些证据转成可训练、可解释的检测信号。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接影响不算大，因为它主要是生成后验证与安全检测，而不是训练目标、架构或数据配比层面的改动。但它对新型语言建模范式，尤其是 diffusion LM，有两个启发。第一，D-LLM 的中间轨迹确实携带比最终输出更丰富的诊断信息，这说明 diffusion 生成过程可以被当作一类可观测的推理/校验轨迹，而不只是采样过程。第二，作者把“轨迹利用”从粗粒度统计推进到 step-layer 级别的证据选择，这对以后做 D-LLM 的训练监控、错误归因、甚至 trajectory-aware reward 或 verifier 设计都有参考价值。贡献量级更像是一个扎实的检测框架，而不是改变预训练范式的工作。","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"实验设置覆盖两个 diffusion LLM、三个 QA benchmark、八个强基线，并做了较完整的消融。全文给出的主结论比较稳定：HIVE 在整体幻觉检测性能上优于基于最终输出不确定性的方法、基于轨迹粗统计的方法，以及不做 step-layer 选择的变体。摘要里没有给出完整数字，正文可见作者重点报告的是 AUROC、AUPRC 一类检测指标，以及结构化验证输出的质量。\n\n消融实验的价值比主表更高。第一类消融是证据来源。只看最终答案隐藏状态，效果明显弱于使用去噪轨迹；只看少数末端 step 也弱于结合变化位置的轨迹证据，说明幻觉信号确实不是只在最后一步出现。第二类消融是选择机制。去掉 step-layer selector、改成平均池化或随机选证据，性能下降，支持“稀疏证据选择”这个核心假设。第三类消融是证据注入方式。prefix conditioning 优于更粗糙的拼接式输入，说明 verifier 更需要结构化地接收隐藏证据，而不是把压缩向量当普通文本特征处理。第四类消融是证据预算与保留步数。作者展示了在较小预算下也能维持较强性能，说明方法不是靠暴力堆轨迹信息取胜。\n\n如果从预训练研究者角度看，最值得注意的实验现象不是“比谁高几个点”，而是：中间去噪状态对错误检测确实有增益，而且这种增益在经过选择后比粗粒度轨迹统计更稳定。这一点支持把 diffusion LM 的 trajectory 当作一等公民来建模。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Figure 1 和 Figure 2，理解作者为什么认为 final-output-only detector 不够，以及 HIVE 的四段式流程。然后重点看 hidden evidence extraction 和 step-layer selection 两节，这两部分决定了方法是否只是“把更多特征喂给 verifier”，还是确实抓住了 D-LLM 的特殊结构。实验部分优先看主表、selector 消融、evidence budget 消融，以及不同 step/layer 贡献分析。如果时间有限，读法可以控制在 25 到 35 分钟：先抓方法图，再看三类消融，最后扫一眼结构化输出案例。真正值得深挖的点有两个：一是 changed-token positions 这个启发式是否足够稳健；二是随机投影压缩是否会丢掉对 verifier 最关键的几何信息。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies hallucination detection for diffusion LLMs, but the real target is not generic uncertainty estimation. It asks how to exploit the denoising trajectory itself. In diffusion text generation, hallucination signals may emerge early, persist across several denoising steps, or fluctuate before the final answer stabilizes. The challenge is therefore to identify a small amount of informative hidden evidence from a large step-by-layer hidden-state tensor, rather than relying only on the final output or coarse trajectory statistics.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, and its direct impact on core pretraining practice is limited. Still, it matters for researchers interested in alternative LM paradigms. It provides concrete evidence that diffusion LMs expose useful intermediate dynamics that can be leveraged for diagnosis, not just generation. That is relevant for trajectory-aware monitoring, verifier design, and possibly future training signals that operate on denoising traces. The contribution is best viewed as a solid analysis-and-verification framework for D-LLMs rather than a change to the modeling paradigm itself.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"$37","reading_guide":"Start with Figure 1 and Figure 2. They explain why final-output-only detectors are insufficient for diffusion LMs and how the four-stage HIVE pipeline is organized. Then read the hidden-evidence extraction and step-layer selection sections carefully; these are the parts that make the method specific to diffusion generation rather than a generic verifier with extra features. In the experiments, prioritize the main comparison table, selector ablations, evidence-budget ablations, and analyses of which steps and layers contribute most. A practical skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. The two points worth deeper inspection are whether the changed-token heuristic is robust enough, and whether random projection preserves the information that matters most for verification.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"别把它当“更好的幻觉检测器”，而是当 D-LLM 轨迹可读出的证据：只看 evidence 选择策略和 verifier 的 prefix-conditioning，能启发训练时的轨迹监督。","en":"Treat it less as a better detector and more as evidence that D-LLM trajectories expose readable signals—skim the evidence selection and prefix-conditioned verifier for ideas on trajectory-level supervision."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Rochester Institute of Technology","NVIDIA Corporation"],"authors":["Guoshenghui Zhao","Weijie Zhao","Tan Yu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","hallucination-detection","trajectory-features","verification","prefix-conditioning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26139","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2604.26139","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2604.26173","title":"Entropy Centroids as Intrinsic Rewards for Test-Time Scaling","abstract":"An effective way to scale up test-time compute of large language models is to sample multiple responses and then select the best one, as in Grok Heavy and Gemini Deep Think. Existing selection methods often rely on external reward models, which requires training a strong reward model and introduces additional computation overhead. As an alternative, previous approaches have explored intrinsic signals, such as confidence and entropy, but these signals are noisy with naive aggregation. In this wor","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-04-28T23:27:21+00:00","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2604_26173.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 test-time scaling 里的一个老问题：当同一个问题采样出多条推理轨迹后，如何不用外部 reward model、也不依赖多数投票，单靠模型自身生成过程中的内在信号，稳定地区分“早期探索但最终收敛”的好轨迹和“后期持续混乱”的坏轨迹。作者认为，问题不在于熵这个信号本身无效，而在于 token 级熵太噪，简单平均又会抹掉时间结构，因此需要一个更粗粒度、带位置信息的 uncertainty 表示。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain objective，但对“模型内部不确定性是否可作为可用训练/推理信号”这个问题给了一个很具体的答案。它的启发主要有三点。第一，token-level entropy 不是只能做 calibration 指标，也可以经过结构化建模后变成 trajectory-level reward。第二，推理质量和不确定性的关系不是单调的：早期高熵可能对应有效探索，后期高熵更像局部崩塌，这一点对 RL、self-training、process reward 设计都有参考意义。第三，它提示了一个方向：如果预训练或后训练改变了熵随时间的分布形态，那么这些动态统计量可能比静态平均置信度更能反映模型能力边界。","technical_approach":"方法核心很简单，但设计上有两层。第一层是把连续高熵 token 段定义成 High Entropy Phase, HEP。作者不是直接对全序列做滑窗平均，而是用双阈值和结束条件把“高熵爆发段”切出来：当 token entropy 超过高阈值时进入 HEP，之后直到连续若干 token 低于低阈值才结束。这样做的目的，是把单 token 的局部抖动压平，得到更接近“一个推理单元处于不确定状态”的片段。第二层是在 HEP 之上构造 Entropy Centroid。每个 HEP 的长度被当作质量，HEP 中点被当作位置，再对整条轨迹做加权平均，得到一个标量，表示高熵探索主要集中在前段还是后段。选择策略非常直接：在 N 条候选轨迹里选 centroid 最低的那条。作者的判断是，正确轨迹往往在前期经历探索，后面进入低熵收敛；错误轨迹则更容易在后段仍然出现高熵混乱。和已有 intrinsic reward 方法相比，它不依赖平均熵、平均置信度或固定窗口统计，而是显式编码了 uncertainty 的时间位置。和 reward model 路线相比，它没有额外训练成本，也不需要任务特定标注。","experiments":"实验覆盖 14B 到 480B 的模型规模，任务包括数学、代码生成、逻辑推理和 agentic task，重点不是单一 benchmark 冲分，而是看 Lowest Centroid 作为统一选择准则是否稳定。全文给出的主结论是：在不同模型尺度和任务类型上，Lowest Centroid 都比已有 intrinsic baselines 更稳，且随着模型变大，收益没有明显消失。作者还做了几类关键分析。其一，HEP 可视化显示高熵 token 确实不是独立噪点，而是成段出现，这为从 token 升到 phase 提供了经验依据。其二，正确与错误轨迹的 centroid 分布有明显分离，正确轨迹的高熵更偏前部。其三，和 token-level entropy、confidence、KL 或固定窗口平均相比，centroid 更能区分“有用探索”和“后期迷失”。从你给出的全文片段看，具体 benchmark 数字没有完整展开，但论文主打的是跨任务一致性和尺度稳定性，而不是某一个数据集上的极端提升幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1、Figure 2，先确认作者到底在建模什么现象：不是“低熵一定好”，而是“高熵出现在什么位置”更重要。然后看 HEP 的正式定义，重点留意高阈值、低阈值、结束条件这几个超参是怎么设的，因为方法是否稳健很大程度取决于 phase 切分是否合理。实验部分建议优先看三类内容：一是不同任务上的主表，判断它是不是只在数学题上有效；二是和 token 平均熵、局部窗口统计的对比；三是可视化分析，尤其是正确/错误轨迹的 centroid 分布。如果只想快速判断是否值得深入，预计 20–30 分钟足够。若进一步深挖，值得想两个问题：这种 temporal uncertainty pattern 是否能反哺 RL 训练；以及 HEP/centroid 是否会受到 tokenizer、长度归一化和 SFT 后 logit sharpening 的系统性影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a selection problem in test-time scaling: given multiple sampled reasoning trajectories, how can we pick the best one without an external reward model and without relying on majority voting? The key claim is that entropy itself is not the issue; the issue is that token-level entropy is too noisy, while simple averaging destroys temporal structure. What matters is not only how uncertain a trajectory is, but when that uncertainty appears along the trajectory.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it turns internal uncertainty dynamics into a usable trajectory-level signal. The main value is conceptual. First, predictive entropy can become a practical intrinsic reward once it is structured properly. Second, the relationship between uncertainty and quality is non-monotonic: early high entropy may reflect productive exploration, while late high entropy often signals failure. That observation matters for RL, process supervision, and self-improvement pipelines. Third, it suggests that temporal statistics of uncertainty may be more informative than static confidence averages when diagnosing how pretraining or post-training changes model behavior.","technical_approach":"$38","experiments":"$39","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the first two figures. In the experiments, focus on three parts: the main cross-task results, the comparison against token-average and window-based baselines, and the visualizations of centroid distributions for correct vs. incorrect trajectories. Models with less than 7B parameters cannot achieve effective compression in the late stage of training; 3. A single agent is usually responsible for code exploration, reasoning about modifications, and producing strictly formatted edits. This mixes broad inspection with precision editing, causing irrelevant code to accumulate in the context window and increasing formatting failures during edit execution.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to coding-agent training and evaluation. This gives a cleaner diagnostic than relying only on end-to-end SWE-bench scores.","technical_approach":"SWE-Edit has a scaffold-level component and a model-level component. At the scaffold level, it splits the standard code editing interface into two subagents. The Viewer takes a file path and a natural-language query, then returns only task-relevant code snippets instead of exposing full file contents to the main agent. Its prompt asks for complete logical blocks and nearby context to reduce the risk of over-pruning. The Editor takes a file path and a natural-language edit instruction, then applies the modification directly. This lets the main agent focus on problem solving and planning, without having to emit brittle find-replace commands. At the model level, the authors train a Qwen3-8B editor with GRPO to choose adaptively between find-replace and whole-file rewrite. Small localized changes favor find-replace, while broader restructuring favors whole-file rewriting.","experiments":"On SWE-bench Verified, the subagent decomposition improves the baseline by 2.1% while reducing inference cost by 17.9%. The GRPO-trained Qwen3-8B editor outperforms single-format editing baselines by selecting the editing mode according to modification complexity. 在一组相关定理上迭代学习、抽象、压缩并沉淀出可复用的 lemma library，从而形成跨题目的能力增长。","importance":"它对基座预训练不是直接贡献，但对“LLM 如何通过交互式过程积累可迁移技能”有明确启发，属于 pretrain↔post-train 接口附近的工作。价值主要有三点。第一，它把 theorem proving 从单次推理问题改成了带外部记忆和知识压缩的持续学习问题。第二，它给出了一种比较具体的 wake-sleep 式 agent 学习框架：wake 阶段负责探索和产生候选知识，sleep 阶段负责抽象、去重、压缩和验证。第三，它说明在高结构化领域里，单纯增加采样和搜索并不如“学会沉淀 reusable abstractions”有效。对做 reasoning pretrain、tool-augmented learning、self-improving agent 的研究员，这个思路比单个 benchmark 提升更值得看。","technical_approach":"DreamProver 的方法围绕一个迭代的 wake–sleep 循环展开。\n\n在 wake stage，系统带着当前 lemma library 去证明训练集中的定理。这里有两个关键步骤。其一是 learnable theorem identification：系统在固定 proof attempt 和 error-correction budget 下，直接提示 LLM 使用当前库中的 lemmas 去证明目标定理。如果在预算内能证明，就把该定理视为当前知识水平下“可学习”的对象，因为它可能蕴含一个建立在现有知识之上的更高层 lemma。其二是 intermediate theorem discovery：对于不能直接证明的难题，系统递归地做 theorem decomposition，把大问题拆成更 tractable 的中间定理。这样得到的中间定理既是证明过程的副产物，也是后续 library 学习的原料。\n\n在 sleep stage，系统不直接把所有候选引理塞进库里，而是做结构化的 lemma evolution。根据引言和方法描述，这一阶段至少包含 clustering、pruning、abstraction、validation 几步：先按语义相似性聚类并去重，避免库无限膨胀；再对同一簇中的具体引理做抽象，生成更一般、更可迁移的高层 lemma；最后验证这些抽象后的 lemma 是否可被 formal system 接受，并用它们更新库。这里的关键取舍很清楚：如果只追求覆盖率，库会迅速变成一堆局部技巧；如果只追求抽象度，又可能生成过泛、不可证或无用的 lemma。DreamProver 试图在可证明性、紧凑性和复用性之间找平衡。\n\n推理时，测试集上的定理证明直接使用学到的 lemma library。也就是说，训练阶段学到的不是参数更新本身，而是一套可显式调用的、经过压缩的形式化知识单元。与很多依赖在线生成中间步骤的方法相比，这种设计更接近“外显技能库”。\n\n从方法差异看，它不同于固定库检索，也不同于每题临时合成中间引理。前者没有成长机制，后者没有沉淀机制。DreamProver 的新意在于把“发现—抽象—验证—复用”做成闭环。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个数学领域 benchmark，包括不等式、数论、组合、平面几何和机器学习理论。作者报告的主结果是：使用学到的 lemma library 后，相比先前方法 proving success rate 提升 61%；同时 proof length 降低 50%，token usage 降低 48%。这组结果说明收益不只是多解出一些题，还体现在证明更短、搜索更省。\n\n从全文可确认的实验重点，至少包括以下几类。第一，跨领域泛化：不是只在单一数学子域上有效，而是在多个 formal domains 上都能受益，这支持“transferable lemma”而非“局部技巧库”的说法。第二，library quality analysis：作者强调 learned library 是 compact 的，说明实验里应有库规模、去重/抽象效果、复用频次之类分析，用来证明 sleep stage 不是形式上的后处理。第三，compute efficiency：proof length 和 token usage 的下降，说明 learned lemmas 改善了搜索路径，而不是单纯靠更大采样预算换成功率。\n\n需要保守说明的是，给出的全文片段没有完整实验表和具体 baseline 名称，因此不宜编造每个数据集上的绝对成功率、库大小或迭代轮数。能可靠引用的数字只有摘要中明确给出的三项：成功率相对提升 61%，证明长度下降 50%，token 使用下降 48%。精读时应重点核对主结果表、wake-sleep 迭代曲线、lemma library 大小与复用率分析，以及去掉 sleep abstraction / clustering / validation 后的消融结果。","reading_guide":"这篇适合从“系统如何积累知识”这个角度读，而不是只盯着 proving pipeline。\n\n先看 Introduction，抓住作者批评的两个对象：固定 lemma library 和 problem-specific intermediate lemmas。前者不能适应新问题分布，后者不能沉淀可迁移知识。理解这点后，wake-sleep 设计就很自然。\n\n然后重点读 Method 里的 wake stage。要看清 learnable theorem identification 的判定标准、proof budget 怎么设、失败后如何进入 recursive decomposition。这里决定了系统收集到的候选 theorem 是偏保守还是偏噪声。\n\n接着读 sleep stage。这里是全文最有信息量的部分，因为真正的贡献不在“又做了一次 proving”，而在“如何把候选引理压缩成可复用库”。建议重点看 clustering/pruning 的表示方式、abstraction 的 prompt 或程序化规则、formal validation 的通过率，以及 library update 策略。\n\n实验部分优先看三类内容：主结果表、迭代轮次带来的性能曲线、library 质量分析。如果时间有限，30 分钟足够抓住主线；如果要判断它对 self-improving reasoning agent 是否有普适意义，需要再看附录里失败案例，尤其是抽象过度、lemma 无法验证、或库膨胀失控的情形。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a persistent limitation in formal theorem proving systems: they can often solve individual problems, but they do not reliably accumulate transferable mid-level knowledge. Existing methods either rely on fixed lemma libraries, which are hard to adapt, or generate problem-specific intermediate lemmas on the fly, which help a single proof but rarely transfer. DreamProver reframes the problem from one-shot proving to iterative knowledge acquisition: how can a proving agent discover, abstract, compress, validate, and reuse lemmas across related theorems so that capability improves over time?","importance":"This is not a direct pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the broader question of how LLM-based systems can accumulate reusable skills through interaction.
